# Skills · S · part 154

949 entries, spearman-correlation-heatmap-clustering to spec-03-implementation-guidance. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spearman-correlation-heatmap-clustering**: Use when several normalization variants (e.g., tGAM, rGAM, rLOESS, QC-RLSC, QC-RSC, SERFF, TIGER) have been applied to the same dataset and you need to visualize how similarly they rank metabolites by differential abundance signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-heatmap-clustering))
- **spearman-correlation-metric-computation**: Use when scoring predicted 3D genome contact maps from models such as Caduceus-Ph or HyenaDNA against observed maps on the DNALONGBENCH contact map prediction task, or when quantifying agreement (SCC, alongside PCC) for specific genomic regions like chr6:145,205,248-145,614,848. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-metric-computation))
- **spearman-correlation-of-factor-loadings**: Use when Tensor-cell2cell (or similar TCA) factor loadings must be compared against a reference decomposition or a context-level phenotype such as COVID-19 severity, and a rank-based, distribution-free agreement metric is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-of-factor-loadings))
- **spearman-correlation-of-ko-abundance-profiles**: Use when a 16S OTU table with a PICRUSt-predicted metagenome must be validated against a shotgun-annotated KO table (e.g. HUMAnN v0.98 KOs), especially across rarefied sequencing depths to find where prediction accuracy converges or crosses over. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-of-ko-abundance-profiles))
- **spearman-correlation-of-marker-expression-with-confidence**: Use when cells carry a nominal annotation over an underlying gradient (e.g. EMT, development) and you need to check that DNN confidence scores track marker gene expression in a monotonic way. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-of-marker-expression-with-confidence))
- **spearman-correlation-omics-behavioral-factors**: Use when relating continuous multi-omics factor loadings (e.g., MOFA factors from methylation/metabolomics) to behavioral dimension scores in a cohort where no family structure needs modeling, and a rank-based association measure is preferred. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-omics-behavioral-factors))
- **spearman-correlation-performance-assessment**: Use when a paired microbiome-metabolome model (e.g. MiMeNet, MelonnPan, NED) has been trained and cross-validated and you need per-metabolite SCC values plus a shuffled-data empirical background to declare which metabolites are well-predicted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-performance-assessment))
- **spearman-correlation-prediction-evaluation**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking metabolite abundance prediction from paired microbiome-metabolome count data and per-metabolite SCC values, mean SCC, and well-predicted metabolite counts are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-prediction-evaluation))
- **spearman-correlation-protein-rna-association-testing**: Use when you have imputed surface protein abundances per cell (from cTP-net on denoised scRNA-seq) and need to test protein-space cell-state structure, e.g., opposing CD56/CD16 gradients separating CD56bright and CD56dim NK subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-protein-rna-association-testing))
- **spearman-correlation-recovery-evaluation**: Use when comparing denoising performance of scRNA-seq methods against a ground-truth full-depth profile, especially to test whether a method that denoises cells independently outperforms neighbor-aggregating methods on rare cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-recovery-evaluation))
- **spearman-correlation-regression-scoring**: Use when a model outputs continuous sgRNA activity/efficiency predictions and you must quantify rank agreement with measured phenotype or activity scores, e.g. validating EpiCas-DL on independent genome-wide screening datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-regression-scoring))
- **spearman-correlation-robustness-assessment**: Use when you need to measure how much adding or deleting a percentage of edges in a regulatory or kinase-substrate network changes downstream activity inference, and whether the change is statistically significant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-robustness-assessment))
- **spearman-correlation-statistical-analysis**: Use when you have paired predicted and observed metabolite abundance vectors from a predictive model (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/spearman-correlation-statistical-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **spearman-correlation-statistical-testing**: Use when comparing regression or classification models for sgRNA activity prediction on datasets like CRISPRoff_tiling (111,682 sgRNAs, 520 genes) and you need rank-based correlation plus significance testing across CV folds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-statistical-testing))
- **spearman-correlation-threshold-derivation**: Use when you need an objective, dataset-specific significance threshold for per-metabolite prediction correlations in a paired microbiome-metabolome regression task, rather than an arbitrary fixed SCC cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-threshold-derivation))
- **spearman-correlation-trajectory-progression**: Use when you have a reconstructed gene regulatory network (CLR/CellNet-style edge list) plus per-cell trajectory progression values from CellRouter and want to rank regulators driving or mediating specific cell-state transitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-trajectory-progression))
- **spearman-correlation-validation**: Use when a single-cell or ensemble-derived continuous genomic track (e.g., compartment eigenvector E1) must be quantitatively validated against a matching bulk assay track on a common genome assembly and bin grid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-validation))
- **spearman-correlation-weight-initialization**: Reference for initializing TF weight matrices when fitting TFvelo gene dynamics models, where candidate TFs (ENCODE/ChEA) need expression-correlation-based starting weights per target gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-weight-initialization))
- **spearman-cross-correlation-lr-tf-covariation**: Use when you have two single-cell trajectories (e.g. EPI and PE lineages from blastoid/blastocyst scRNA-seq ordered by monocle3), a ligand-receptor pair list, and RTN master-regulator results, and need to find co-varying LR–TF pairs prior to Granger causal testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-cross-correlation-lr-tf-covariation))
- **spearman-distance-k-medoids-clustering**: Use when you have simulated (or real) per-feature expression/accessibility profiles across multiple cell types and need to verify that features with shared simulated regulatory programs group into coherent profile clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-distance-k-medoids-clustering))
- **spearman-distance-kmedoids-clustering**: Use when validating that a single-cell multi-omics simulation (e.g. sc_mosim scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq output) reproduced the intended cell-type-specific expression profiles, by clustering gene profiles and measuring misassignment rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spearman-distance-kmedoids-clustering))
- **spearman-fold-difference-correlation-analysis**: Use when validating inferred miRNA activity against measured single-cell or purified-cell-type miRNA profiles, by correlating predicted vs observed fold-differences between cell-type pairs across miRNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-fold-difference-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-global-distance-correlation-analysis**: Use when benchmarking SC-to-ST spatial reconstruction methods (e.g., CellContrast vs SageNet, CeLEry, CytoSpace, NovoSpaRc, Tangram) on test sections with truth locations, to quantify global distance-structure recovery alongside neighbor-hit and JSD metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-global-distance-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-injection-order-analysis**: Use when a quantified concentration table from a targeted LC-MS/MS sequence (e.g. porcine plasma) must be checked for residual injection-order drift after applying a drift-correction strategy, or when comparing correction strategies for compound-specific residual drift. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-injection-order-analysis))
- **spearman-pearson-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing read-level coverage tracks of synthetic vs real scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq data binned into genomic windows (e.g., 1000-bp bins on chr1) to quantify simulator fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-pearson-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-rank-correlation**: Use when you have per-sample gene-signature scores and a continuous per-sample metadata variable (purity/VAF) in a pdacR dataset and need a rank-based association with rho, p-value, and n. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-accuracy-assessment**: Use when benchmarking DRS or long-read transcript quantification tools against spike-in controls with known concentrations (e.g. SIRV E2), to rank tools by rank-preserving accuracy via Spearman's rho. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-accuracy-assessment))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when validating Nanodynamo inference on simulated RNA life-cycle data by correlating modelled vs expected RNA species abundances and inferred vs expected synthesis, processing, degradation, export, and translation rates per gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when a per-pairing benchmark metrics table exists (genus- and species-level sensitivity, specificity, error score, alpha diversity deviation for each profiler-library pairing) and the goal is to test whether quality traits are correlated across pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when a per-cell matrix (e.g. MORPHnMOL.csv from a SpaceM scAnalysis run) contains both normalized lipid intensities and microscopy-derived fluorescence such as LD540, and the goal is to find lipids positively or negatively correlated with that readout across single cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have pairwise dissimilarity matrices from multiple distance measures and an independent reference phylogenetic/evolutionary distance set, and need to rank which measures recover known evolutionary relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--f26ea1883ac34aea))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when per-sample phylogenetic novelty scores (e.g., NSTI from a 16S marker gene tree) are paired with per-sample prediction accuracy and a monotonic rank-correlation trend must be quantified, globally and per habitat. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--c8e5c3cb54c084db))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when pseudobulk 3'UTR isoform counts from scRNA-seq samples (e.g., FACS-sorted HSCs or ESCs) need to be benchmarked against bulk 3' end sequencing TPMs via rank correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--c19cb3b8693084ae))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have ranked gene lists from two or more SVG/HVG identification methods on the same dataset and need to quantify rank agreement via Spearman correlations plus top-n (n = 10, 20, 50, 100, 200) overlap counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--d860f6d0c9ce1e1c))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing pseudotime (or any ranked) orderings between a full single-cell dataset and repeated random cell subsamples, to measure ordering robustness of trajectory-inference methods such as DTFLOW, Scanpy, or Monocle2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--68fdb39a870e49a7))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when a multimodal single-cell dataset (e.g., CITE-seq RNA + ADT) is available and you need to test whether an imputation or transformation in one modality makes its pairwise cell distances more consistent with distances in the other modality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--bf80c9960238e507))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have per-node scores (e.g., scBONITA node importance from rule inference on KEGG networks) plus graph-derived node statistics and need to test their association via Spearman rank correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-rank-correlation-analysis--04cccf94ebdfb9b8))
- **spearman-rank-correlation-computation**: Use when two per-record attributes from a structured catalog must be compared by rank — e.g. an urgency/consequence rank versus a binary evidence-strength indicator — to quantify their association. - **spearman-rank-correlation-computation**: Use when a continuous or ordinal data matrix and response are given and you need fast, robust, nonparametric quantification of feature-response and feature-pair associations to feed a correlation-based filter (RRCT, mRMR_Spearman). - **spearman-rank-correlation-statistics**: Use when you need to test whether a monotonic relationship exists between an ordinal/dosage variable (e.g. duplication copy number across strain pairs) and a continuous outcome (e.g. log2 expression induction), without assuming linearity or normality. - **spec-00-scope-split**: 當使用者輸入 /spec-00 [path]、/spec-run（帶來新需求時），或說「評估需求大小」「這個需求要不要切」「幫我切分需求」「拆成幾個模組」時使用。在 spec-01 之前評估一份新需求的大小：未超過門檻就以單一模組繼續；超過門檻就自動決定切分方案並落盤，只有不確定之處會改變下一個要跑的模組範圍時才停下來問，其餘疑點放進待人檢視清單、不阻擋流程。之後 spec-01 一次只掃一個模組。 ([yi-luan/specengine-plugin](https://github.com/yi-luan/specengine-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/spec-00-scope-split) · ★11)
